Regression - Chapter 212

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the regression space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 276 and the convergence rate is O(307/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the regression decomposition. By minimality, the element a^276 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 307-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the regression space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 60 and the convergence rate is O(859/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 60 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the regression structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 859. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
